def storage:
  stor0 is address at storage 0
  stor0_160 is uint24 at storage 0 offset 160
  stor0_184 is uint16 at storage 0 offset 184
  stor0_200 is uint16 at storage 0 offset 200
  stor0_216 is uint16 at storage 0 offset 216
  stor0_232 is uint8 at storage 0 offset 232
  stor0_240 is uint8 at storage 0 offset 240
  feeGrowthGlobal0X128 is uint256 at storage 1
  feeGrowthGlobal1X128 is uint256 at storage 2
  stor3 is uint128 at storage 3
  stor3_128 is uint128 at storage 3 offset 128
  liquidity is uint128 at storage 4
  ticks is mapping of struct of 4 slots at storage 5
  stor6 is mapping of uint256 at storage 6
  positions is mapping of struct of 4 slots at storage 7
  stor8 is fixed array of 65535 struct of 1 slot at storage 8

def liquidity(): # not payable
  return liquidity

def feeGrowthGlobal1X128(): # not payable
  return feeGrowthGlobal1X128

def positions(bytes32 _param1): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >= 32
  return uint128(positions[_param1].field_0), 
         positions[_param1].field_256,
         positions[_param1].field_512,
         uint128(positions[_param1].field_768),
         positions[_param1].field_896

def tickBitmap(int16 _param1): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >= 32
  return stor6[int16(_param1)]

def feeGrowthGlobal0X128(): # not payable
  return feeGrowthGlobal0X128

def ticks(int24 _param1): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >= 32
  return uint128(ticks[int24(_param1)].field_0), 
         int128(ticks[int24(_param1)].field_128),
         ticks[int24(_param1)].field_256,
         ticks[int24(_param1)].field_512,
         int56(uint56(ticks[int24(_param1)].field_768)),
         address(ticks[int24(_param1)].field_824),
         uint32(ticks[int24(_param1)].field_984),
         bool(ticks[int24(_param1)].field_1016)
